                                                                   Easiest Spicy Cod
Ingredients                         Directions
1 1/2 pounds cod fillets           Preheat oven to 350 degrees F (175 degrees C).
2 cups salsa
2 tablespoons chopped fresh        Rinse and dry cod fillets. Place fillets in a lightly greased casserole
parsley                            dish. Pour salsa over fish. Sprinkle with parsley, salt and pepper.
salt and pepper to taste
                                   Bake in preheated oven for 30 minutes. Serve warm over rice.

1 cup finely chopped onion

sauce

flavoring (optional)

                                                Spicy Garlic Lime Chicken
Ingredients                       Directions
3/4 teaspoon salt                In a small bowl, mix together salt, black pepper, cayenne, paprika,
1/4 teaspoon black pepper        1/4 teaspoon garlic powder, onion powder, thyme and parsley.
1/4 teaspoon cayenne pepper      Sprinkle spice mixture generously on both sides of chicken breasts.
1/8 teaspoon paprika
1/4 teaspoon garlic powder       Heat butter and olive oil in a large heavy skillet over medium heat.
1/8 teaspoon onion powder        Saute chicken until golden brown, about 6 minutes on each side.
1/4 teaspoon dried thyme         Sprinkle with 2 teaspoons garlic powder and lime juice. Cook 5
                                 minutes, stirring frequently to coat evenly with sauce.
1/4 teaspoon dried parsley
4 boneless, skinless chicken
breast halves

2 tablespoons butter
1 tablespoon olive oil
2 teaspoons garlic powder
3 tablespoons lime juice

                                                                 Spicy Onion Rings
Ingredients                        Directions
4 large sweet onions, peeled and   Place the onions, egg substitute, and flour into separate shallow
sliced into thick rings            bowls. In another shallow bowl, stir together the bread crumbs, salt,
1 cup egg substitute               pepper, cayenne, oregano, basil, and red pepper flakes.
1 cup all-purpose flour
2 cups bread crumbs                Heat oil in a heavy skillet or deep-fryer to 365 degrees F (180
1 teaspoon salt                    degrees C). Dip rings of onion into flour, then into the egg
2 teaspoons ground black pepper    substitute, and then into the bread crumb mixture, shaking off any
                                   excess after each dip. Carefully drop each onion ring into the hot
1 1/2 teaspoons cayenne pepper
                                   oil, and fry for about 30 seconds on each side, or until golden.
1 teaspoon dried oregano
                                   Remove from hot oil to paper towels to drain. Serve hot with lots of
1 teaspoon dried basil             ketchup.
2 teaspoons red pepper flakes
1 quart canola oil for frying

                                                                   Spicy Orange Beef
Ingredients                         Directions
1/4 cup orange juice concentrate    In a small bowl, combine the first six ingredients; set aside. In a
3 tablespoons soy sauce             large skillet or wok, heat 3 tablespoons oil over medium heat; saute
3 tablespoons water                 garlic for 30 seconds. Add broccoli, onions, ginger and pepper
1 tablespoon cornstarch             flakes; stir-fry for 2 minutes or until broccoli is crisp-tender. Remove
1 tablespoon finely grated orange   vegetables and keep warm. Heat remaining oil in skillet; add beef.
peel                                Stir-fry until no longer pink. Stir orange juice mixture; add to skillet.
1 teaspoon sugar                    Cook and stir for 2 minutes or until sauce is thickened. Return
                                    vegetables to pan. Add oranges and heat through. Serve over rice.
5 tablespoons vegetable oil,
divided
2 garlic cloves, minced
4 cups broccoli florets
12 green onions cut into 1-inch

                         Spicy Roast Goose with Apple Stuffing
Ingredients                          Directions
1 (12 pound) goose, thawed if        Wash the goose thoroughly and pat dry with paper towels. Let the
purchased frozen                     goose sit at room temperature for about 30 minutes. Meanwhile,
1/4 teaspoon ground allspice         mix together the allspice, 1/2 teaspoon cinnamon, salt and pepper
1/2 teaspoon ground cinnamon         in a small bowl and set aside to make a spice rub.
salt and black pepper to taste
8 apples, peeled, cored and sliced   Remove any lumps of visible fat from the cavity of the goose, and
1/2 cup Calvados (apple brandy)      render the goose fat in a skillet over medium heat until the fat is
                                     liquid and the remaining bits are crisp and brown, about 15 minutes.
2 teaspoons ground cinnamon
                                     Reserve 3 tablespoons of goose fat and save the rest for another
                                     use, or discard.

                                     Working in batches if necessary, fry the sliced apples in the
                                     reserved goose fat in a large skillet over medium heat until the apple
                                     slices are browned, about 10 minutes per batch. Remove the
                                     browned apple slices to a large mixing bowl, let cool slightly, and
                                     sprinkle with the Calvados and 2 teaspoons of cinnamon. Mix
                                     lightly to combine.

                                     With the point of a sharp knife, prick the entire skin of the goose,
                                     being careful not to cut into the meat. Rub the exterior of the goose
                                     well with the spice rub, massaging the spices into the skin.

                                     Preheat an oven to 400 degrees F (200 degrees C).

                                     Spoon the apple stuffing lightly into the cavity of the goose, and lay
                                     it on a large sheet of aluminum foil big enough to wrap over the
                                     entire goose. Wrap the goose in the foil, seal the edges, and make
                                     a 1/2-inch hole in the bottom of the foil packet to allow the fat from
                                     the goose to drain. Place the bird breast side up, on a rack set into
                                     a large baking pan, and roast the goose in the preheated oven until
                                     no longer pink at the bone and the juices run clear, about an hour
                                     and a half. Peel the foil back from the breast meat and roast for
                                     another 45 minutes until the skin browns, basting once or twice to
                                     prevent burning. An instant-read thermometer inserted into the
                                     thickest part of the thigh, near the bone should read 180 degrees F
                                     (82 degrees C).

                                     Remove the goose from the oven, cover with a doubled sheet of
                                     aluminum foil, and allow to rest in a warm area 10 to 15 minutes
                                     before serving.

                                     Combine vinegar, apple juice, brown sugar, salt, black pepper,

                                     mustard, allspice, cumin, and cayenne pepper in a large non-
1/2 teaspoon ground allspice         reactive pan over medium heat; cook and stir until sugar dissolves

1/4 teaspoon cayenne pepper          boil; reduce heat and simmer, stirring frequently, until liquid has
1 lemon, quartered and sliced thin   reduced by half, 30 to 45 minutes. Add the lemon slices and cook
                                     another 15 minutes.

                                     Sterilize the jars and lids in boiling water for at least 5 minutes. Pack
                                     the jam into the hot, sterilized jars, filling the jars to within 1/4 inch
                                     of the top. Run a knife or a thin spatula around the insides of the
                                     jars after they have been filled to remove any air bubbles. Wipe the
                                     rims of the jars with a moist paper towel to remove any food
                                     residue. Top with lids, and screw on rings.

                                     Place a rack in the bottom of a large stockpot and fill halfway with
                                     water. Bring to a boil over high heat, then carefully lower the jars
                                     into the pot using a holder. Leave a 2 inch space between the jars.
                                     Pour in more boiling water if necessary until the water level is at
                                     least 1 inch above the tops of the jars. Bring the water to a full boil,
                                     cover the pot, and process for 30 minutes.

                                     Remove the jars from the stockpot and place onto a cloth-covered
                                     or wood surface, several inches apart, until cool. Once cool, press
                                     the top of each lid with a finger, ensuring that the seal is tight (lid
                                     does not move up or down at all). Store in a cool, dark area.
